shared_focus = {
	id = ENG_The_National_Corporate_Support_Bill
	icon = national_support_bill_icon
	cost = 5
	x = 22
	y = 18
	prerequisite = {
		focus = ENG_English_Industry
	}
	completion_reward = {
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				free_building_slots = {

					size > 0

				}
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
			set_state_flag = GGR_Company_Pacts_civ
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				free_building_slots = {
					building = industrial_complex

				}
